Experiments across video understanding and reasoning benchmarks show that $\textbf{Video-OPSD}$ consistently improves upon Standard OPSD across multiple backbones and achieves performance comparable to GRPO while requiring substantially less training time, establishing an effective and efficient post-training approach for Video-LLMs.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond Classification: Task-Dependent Learnability under Privacy-Motivated Image Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27066)
*Leon Ranke,Wolfgang Hübner,Ronny Hug,Michael Arens,Jürgen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算感知的多任务评估协议，用于更全面地评估计算机视觉中的隐私增强技术（PETs）。该协议结合轻量级代理任务来评估视觉结构的互补方面，证明仅依赖图像分类评估PETs过于简单，因为分类准确率相似的PETs在其他任务上可能表现差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PETs评估主要依赖图像分类准确率，但分类任务仅捕捉语义可分离性，对几何、空间布局或局部边界变化鲁棒，无法作为通用视觉任务的充分代理。而穷举下游任务评估计算成本过高，因为每种PET变换和参数设置都需要训练模型。

Method: 提出一个计算感知的多任务协议，包含轻量级代理任务，这些任务针对视觉结构的互补方面（如语义、空间布局、局部边界等），同时保持简单和快速计算。协议应用于三类PETs：不可逆隐私变换、基于密钥的块基元和学习型图像加密方案。

Result: 实验表明，在分类准确率相似的PETs，在其他视觉任务上的表现可能差异显著，证明了仅基于分类报告的PET评估协议不足，需要超越分类-only的评估方法。

Conclusion: PETs评估应超越分类-only报告，采用多任务的轻量级协议来提供更全面的性能评估，在计算效率与评估充分性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Privacy-Enhancing Technologies (PETs) in computer vision often rely on noise or image perturbations to protect visual data while securely processing it, creating a trade-off between task performance and protection. This trade-off is commonly evaluated using image classification, which primarily captures semantic separability and remains robust despite significant geometric, spatial layout or local boundary alterations. As a result, it is too simplistic as a proxy for generic vision tasks. Exhaustive downstream-task evaluation, however, is computationally expensive because models must often be trained for each PET transformation and parameter setting. We therefore propose a compute-aware multi-task protocol for evaluating PETs in model training. It combines lightweight proxy tasks that target complementary aspects of visual structure while remaining simple and fast to compute. Across irreversible privacy transformations, key-based block primitives, and learnable image encryption schemes, we demonstrate that PETs with similar classification accuracy can differ substantially on other tasks. The outcomes highlight the need for PET evaluation protocols that move beyond classification-only reporting.

</details>


### [68] [SpatialCrafter: Single Image World Modeling with Generative 3D Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27073)
*Chuan Fang,Lingteng Qiu,Yixun Liang,Rui Chen,Kunming Luo,Zhaohua Zheng,Tongyuan Bai,Feipeng Tian,Zilong Dong,Zihan Zhou,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialCrafter是一个两阶段图像到场景生成框架，通过引入全局3D代理（proxy）来解决现有基于视频扩散模型方法中的随机幻觉、长期漂移和3D一致性差的问题。框架分为全局代理生成（PaSS Flow模块预测点锚定稀疏结构）和外观细化（将VDM重构为Generative Deferred Refiner），并引入Parallel Geometry Injection和Proxy-Aware Corruption训练策略。同时构建了首个包含115K场景的混合数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视频扩散模型的图像到场景生成方法依赖不完整的条件信号（如稀疏点云或2D全景图），导致生成结果存在随机幻觉、长期漂移和3D一致性不佳的问题，且缺乏适合该任务的训练数据集。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 全局代理生成阶段，通过Point-anchored Sparse Structure (PaSS) Flow模块预测空间对齐且几何一致的3D代理；(2) 外观细化阶段，将视频扩散模型重新框架为Generative Deferred Refiner，在代理定义的场景几何上合成高频照片级细节。并引入Parallel Geometry Injection和Proxy-Aware Corruption两种训练策略增强鲁棒性，同时构建包含115K场景的混合数据集。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SpatialCrafter优于现有最先进方法，能缓解长期漂移问题，并在快速相机运动和极端视角变化下保持鲁棒性和一致性。

Conclusion: 通过引入全局3D代理将生成过程解耦为代理生成与外观细化，SpatialCrafter有效解决了现有方法在图像到场景生成中的一致性问题，并提供了首个大规模混合数据集和可复现的开源实现。

Abstract: Explorable image-to-scene generation is essential for applications in gaming, robotics, and virtual reality. Existing methods based on video diffusion model (VDM) commonly rely on incomplete conditioning signals such as sparse point clouds or 2D panoramas, leading to stochastic hallucinations, long-term drifts and suboptimal 3D consistency. We present SpatialCrafter, a novel two-stage framework that addresses these issues by introducing a global 3D proxy for high-fidelity image-to-scene generation. Specifically, we decompose the generation process into global proxy generation and appearance refinement. For proxy generation, we propose a Point-anchored Sparse Structure~(PaSS) Flow module that predicts a spatially aligned and geometrically consistent 3D proxy. For appearance refinement, we re-frame the VDM as a Generative Deferred Refiner which synthesizes high-frequency photorealistic details upon proxy-defined scene geometry. To better integrate the proxy with the pre-trained VDM, we introduce Parallel Geometry Injection and Proxy-Aware Corruption training strategies, which improve robustness to proxy artifacts without disrupting the pretrained generative manifold. Furthermore, as no suitable dataset exists for this explorable scene generation task, we construct a new large-scale dataset of 115K scenes. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first hybrid dataset for image-to-scene generation. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that SpatialCrafter outperforms state-of-the-art methods, mitigates long-term drift, and remains robust and consistent under rapid camera motion and extreme viewpoint changes. Code, models, and the newly constructed dataset will be publicly released. See more at https://fangchuan.github.io/SpatialCrafter/.

</details>


### [69] [Automated 2D and 3D Segmentation of AMD and DME Lesions in OCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27095)
*Lucia Sundberg,Zhihao Zhao,M. Ali Nasseri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对AMD和DME两种致盲性眼病，开发并系统消融了四种OCT病灶分割流程（2D与3D变体），在域内验证集上达到Dice 0.76-0.82及良好的体积与表面校准。通过消融确立了全量程、校准感知的采用标准，并发现集成组合是提升性能最一致的因素。在缺乏病灶级标注的外部临床队列OLIVES上，利用代理指标框架（生物标志物AUROC、CST相关性、纵向一致性）评估泛化性，结果显示预测在外部分布外仍能跟踪临床生物标志物（虽弱于域内），为自动化病灶负荷追踪作为临床工具提供了证据而非验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数OCT深度分割模型仅在域内验证，缺乏对跨采集协议、不同临床数据分布泛化能力的系统评估。由于AMD和DME是主要致盲原因，且治疗决策依赖于对细微病灶的精准检测，验证分割模型在真实外部临床数据上的表现对临床落地至关重要。

Method: 开发并系统消融四种病灶分割流程（2D/3D × AMD/DME），通过全量程、校准感知的消融标准进行机制分析。在外部队列OLIVES上（无病灶级标注），构建基于生物标志物AUROC、中央子区域厚度（CST）相关性和纵向一致性的代理指标框架，评估跨分布泛化能力。

Result: 四种流程在域内验证集上Dice达到0.76-0.82，体积与表面校准系数均≥0.97。消融发现集成组合是最稳定的性能提升驱动因素；全量程校准感知评估能捕获切片级评估遗漏的机制。外部评估显示预测能追踪临床生物标志物，但关联强度弱于域内。

Conclusion: 外部临床队列证据表明OCT病灶分割模型具备一定跨分布泛化能力，能跟踪临床生物标志物，但外部性能下降说明不能将其视为临床工具的有效验证。研究同时展示了消融和分析方法学贡献（全量程校准感知标准与代理指标框架），为临床转化提供了更审慎的证据路径。

Abstract: Age-related macular degeneration (AMD) and diabetic macular edema (DME) are leading causes of vision loss, and optical coherence tomography (OCT) is the standard modality for detecting and monitoring the subtle lesions that drive treatment decisions. Most deep-learning segmentation work for OCT is validated only in-domain, leaving generalization to clinical data collected under different acquisition protocols largely untested. This work develops and systematically ablates four lesion-segmentation pipelines -- 2D and 3D variants for AMD and DME -- reaching Dice scores of 0.76 to 0.82 with strong volumetric and surface calibration (r vol, r surf greater than or equal to 0.97 across all four pipelines) on an in-domain validation set. The ablation process establishes a full-volume, calibration-aware adoption standard that catches mechanisms an ordinary slice-level evaluation would keep, and identifies ensemble composition as the most consistent driver of improvement. To test generalization, the models are evaluated on OLIVES, an external clinical cohort with no lesion-level ground truth, using a proxy-metric framework built around biomarker AUROC, central subfield thickness (CST) correlation, and longitudinal concordance. Predictions track clinical biomarkers outside the training distribution, though less strongly than in-domain -- evidence for, not validation of, automated lesion-burden tracking as a clinical tool.

</details>


### [70] [EditaLive! Unified Character Video Editing for Live Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27123)
*Zhiyuan Li,Chi-Man Pun,Peng-Tao Jiang,Bo Li,Xiaodong Cun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditaLive是一个用于实时流媒体角色视频编辑的新型框架，基于预训练图像动画模型Wan-Animate，通过CharEdit-50K数据集进行指令化人类中心视频编辑的微调，并采用对齐的自展开蒸馏策略将模型压缩为两步采样器，实现忠实表情保持和低延迟实时流推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法主要针对场景级内容，而直播场景更关注人类主体。直接应用现有方法存在面部表情不一致问题，且依赖多次离线推理步骤，不适合实时交互。

Method: 1) 以预训练图像动画模型Wan-Animate为基础，其天然解耦外观与运动，通过参考帧编辑和视频重建，利用收集的CharEdit-50K数据集将其改造为指令化人类中心视频编辑模型；2) 将模型从离线双向适配为因果流式生成；3) 设计对齐的自展开蒸馏策略，将模型压缩为两步采样器，其中固定RoPE和对齐强制减少训练-推理差异，首帧保持的稀疏注意力过滤冗余历史信息以缓解外观漂移。

Result: 大量实验表明，EditaLive在编辑性能上达到最先进水平，同时忠实保持面部表情并实现低延迟实时流推理。

Conclusion: EditaLive成功解决了直播场景下人类中心视频编辑的实时性与表情一致性挑战，为实时流式角色视频编辑提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Conventional video editing primarily focuses on scene-level content, whereas live streaming places greater emphasis on the human subject. However, directly applying existing video-editing methods to human-centric live streaming remains challenging, as they may introduce facial-expression inconsistencies and typically depend on multiple offline inference steps, making them unsuitable for real-time interaction. We propose EditaLive, a novel framework for real-time streaming character video editing. In detail, we start from a pretrained image animation model (Wan-Animate), which naturally decouples appearance from motion, and repurpose it as the base model for instruction-based human-centric video editing by reference frame editing and video reconstruction via the collected CharEdit-50K dataset. Besides, we adapt the model from offline bidirectional to causal streaming generation, and design an aligned self-rollout distillation strategy that compresses the model into a two-step sampler, where fixed RoPE and align forcing reduce training--inference discrepancies, and first-frame preserved sparse attention filters redundant historical information to mitigate appearance drift. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EditaLive delivers state-of-the-art editing performance with faithful preservation of facial expressions and low-latency real-time streaming inference.

</details>


### [71] [ReViCo: Unveiling the Limitations of VLMs in Visual Text Understanding via Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27154)
*Bojun Zhang,Junhong Liang,Feifei Zhai,Fengxian Ji,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReViCo（真实视觉校正）基准，通过视觉文本纠错这一新任务评估视觉语言模型（VLM）对图像中文本的深层理解能力，实验表明即使最先进的VLM与人类表现仍有显著差距，多数模型难以准确感知视觉文本导致频繁纠错错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM虽然在通用视觉任务上表现出色，但在深入理解图像中的文本信息方面仍存在明显不足。缺乏一个专门的基准来系统评估VLM对视觉文本的深度理解能力，特别是对文本与其视觉上下文交互关系的理解。

Method: 提出ReViCo基准，通过真实世界图像的视觉文本纠错任务评估VLM。采用两种评估范式：基于提示（prompt-based）的策略和针对性的模型训练（targeted model training），系统性地评测多种VLM模型。

Result: 实验发现即使性能最好的VLM与人类表现之间仍存在显著差距；大多数模型难以准确感知视觉文本内容，从而导致频繁的纠错错误。

Conclusion: ReViCo作为一个新的基准，揭示了当前VLM在视觉文本深度理解上的严重不足，为开发更鲁棒、更具文本感知能力的VLM提供了基准基础。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown great success in general visual tasks, yet they still struggle to deeply understand text within images. In this paper, we introduce ReViCo (Real Visual Correction), a benchmark designed to evaluate VLM text understanding through a novel task of visual text error correction. ReViCo challenges models to identify and fix text errors in real-world images, which requires a profound understanding of the interplay between visual text and its surrounding visual context. We benchmark various VLMs using two distinct paradigms: prompt-based strategy and targeted model training, both aimed at pushing the limits of current models. Our experiments reveal a striking performance gap between even the best VLMs and human, and further analysis also shows that most models struggle to accurately perceive the visual text, resulting in frequent correction errors. By highlighting these gaps, ReViCo provides a new benchmark foundation for developing more robust and text-aware VLMs.

</details>


### [72] [Magpie: Real-Time World Renderer for Interactive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27168)
*Xiaoyu Zhan,Xinyu Wang,Xiaohong Zhang,Huanjie Zhu,Tengjiao Sun,Pengcheng Fang,Jiaxing Yu,Yanwen Guo,Dongjie Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Magpie是一个面向交互式游戏的实时生成式世界渲染系统。它将游戏逻辑执行与视觉生成分离：游戏引擎负责解析玩家操作并维护世界状态，独立的渲染服务器基于引擎产生的白盒帧生成视觉输出。该系统为生成模型应用于实时游戏渲染提供了系统级实现路径，保留了游戏玩法的可设计性和可复现性，同时降低了早期游戏原型对完整视觉资产的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统游戏开发依赖传统图形管线，高质量视觉内容需要建模、材质、动画、光照、特效及运行时优化，导致资产生产成本高、开发周期长。虽然视频基础模型改变了影视制作，但游戏不同于线性媒体，需要连续逼真的画面，同时还要保证稳定的可复现玩法规则、对象状态和交互结果，因此需要探索将生成模型应用于实时游戏渲染的方案。

Method: Magpie将游戏执行与视觉生成解耦：设计师在游戏引擎中定义场景和规则；运行时游戏引擎解析玩家操作并维护世界状态；独立的渲染服务器基于引擎生成的白盒帧产生视觉输出。通过这种系统级架构，生成模型只负责视觉渲染部分，而核心玩法逻辑仍由传统引擎确定性执行。

Result: Magpie提供了将生成模型应用于实时游戏渲染的系统级实现路径，实现了实时生成式世界渲染，同时保留了游戏玩法的可设计性和可复现性，并减少了早期游戏原型对完整视觉资产的依赖。

Conclusion: Magpie通过分离游戏玩法和视觉生成，成功将生成式模型引入实时游戏渲染领域，在保证玩法稳定可复现的前提下降低了传统视觉资产制作成本，为游戏行业的生成式渲染提供了新的系统实现方案。

Abstract: Modern game development relies heavily on conventional graphics pipelines. High-quality visual content requires modeling, material authoring, animation, lighting, effects, and runtime optimization, making asset production expensive and extending the development cycle of game prototypes. Recently, video foundation models are beginning to change film and video production, but games differ from linear media, they require not only continuous and realistic imagery, but also stable and reproducible gameplay rules, object states, and interaction outcomes. We present Magpie, a real-time generative world-rendering system for interactive games. Magpie separates gameplay execution from visual generation. Designers define scenes and rules in a game engine. At runtime, the Game Engine resolves player actions and maintains world state, while an independent Render Server generates visual output from white-box frames produced by the engine. Magpie provides a system-level implementation path for applying generative models to real-time game rendering. It preserves gameplay designability and reproducibility, and reduces the dependence of early game prototypes on complete visual assets.

</details>


### [73] [Ancient-Bench: A Comprehensive Multi-millennial, Multi-medium, and Multi-script Benchmark for Ancient Chinese Artifact Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27169)
*Hiuyi Cheng,Nuo Xu,Yuyi Zhang,Xuhan Zheng,Wei Pan,Jing Zhang,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Yihua Teng,Jihao Wu,Haoyu Ren,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ancient-Bench是一个涵盖2,700张古代中国文物文字图像的综合基准数据集，包含跨越3,000年文字演化的多千年维度、覆盖九类文物的多媒质维度以及涵盖七种历史书体的多字体维度，并定义了三种适配异质媒质特征的标注标准，实验揭示通用视觉语言模型和OCR专用模型在古文识别上仍面临变体字、特殊符号和幻觉等未解挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有古文识别基准存在"碎片化"问题，表现为时间覆盖有限、媒质多样性不足、字体类型不完整，难以全面评估模型在古代中国文物文字识别方面的能力，阻碍了文物数字化进程。

Method: 构建包含2,700张图像的综合基准数据集Ancient-Bench，从三个维度设计：时间维度跨越3,000年文字演化，媒质维度覆盖九类文物，字体维度涵盖七种历史书体；同时针对异质媒质定义了符号标准化、字符标准化和解析标准化三种标注标准，以支持一致公平的评估。

Result: 在Ancient-Bench上对通用视觉语言模型（VLMs）和OCR专用模型的广泛实验表明，古代中国文物文字识别仍未被根本解决，在变体字符、专门符号和幻觉方面存在持续性挑战。

Conclusion: 古文文物文字识别仍是一个开放性问题，Ancient-Bench为社区提供了结构化基准以推动该领域研究，数据集已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Ancient Chinese artifact text recognition is fundamental to heritage digitization, and benchmarks for ancient texts are essential for evaluating current model capabilities. However, existing benchmarks suffer from ''fragmentation'', manifested in limited temporal coverage, limited medium diversity, and incomplete script types. Therefore, we present Ancient-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of 2,700 images for ancient Chinese artifact text recognition, featuring three dimensions: Multi-millennial (spanning 3,000 years of character evolution), Multi-medium (covering nine artifact categories), and Multi-script (encompassing seven historical script forms). To enable consistent and fair evaluation across heterogeneous media, we further define three annotation standards tailored to the medium-specific characteristics of ancient texts: symbol standardization, character standardization, and parsing standardization. Extensive experiments on Ancient-Bench covering general Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and OCR-specialist models reveal that ancient Chinese artifact text recognition remains fundamentally unsolved, with persistent challenges in variant characters, specialized symbols, and hallucination. The dataset is available at https://github.com/SCUT-DLVCLab/Ancient_Bench.

</details>


### [74] [Temporal Sensitivity Analysis of Tessera Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27175)
*Julia Guerrero-Viu,Alex López-Cifuentes,Ignacio Pérez-Villar,Fabio Pacifici*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对地球观测基础模型Tessera在土地覆盖制图中的时间敏感性进行了受控研究。通过冻结编码器，在不同观测窗口（从全年到单日）下重算嵌入，并用线性探针和UNet分割头进行基准测试。研究发现嵌入价值与任务相关：在依赖物候区分类别（如PASTIS-R作物类型）时嵌入优势显著，在时间稳定类别（如森林）中两者在充分监督下持平，但嵌入仍更具标签效率。时间窗口缩短导致的性能下降是渐进的且依赖类别，单日嵌入在LUCAS上仍达到随机水平的3.4倍。研究结论是时间覆盖是可调成本而非固定前提，为近实时制图等场景开辟了新可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测应用需要既精确又频繁更新的土地覆盖地图，但现有最强的地球观测基础模型都是基于全年观测构建嵌入。这引出一个关键问题：嵌入是否必须依赖全年观测？研究旨在通过受控实验量化Tessera模型嵌入对观测窗口长度的敏感性，以探索缩短观测周期、实现近实时制图的可能性。

Method: 研究采用受控实验设计：冻结Tessera编码器，在不同观测窗口（全年递减至单日）下重计算嵌入，输入到线性探针和UNet分割头中，并与从零训练的模型在LUCAS、DynamicEarthNet和PASTIS-R三个数据集上进行对比基准测试。

Result: 嵌入价值具有任务依赖性：在PASTIS-R上mIoU达58.3，比最优从零模型高46%；在时间稳定类别上两者充分监督下持平，但嵌入表现更佳的标签效率。窗口从一年缩短至一个月，PASTIS-R准确率损失39%，DynamicEarthNet仅损失5%；单日嵌入在LUCAS上仍达随机水平3.4倍的分类性能。

Conclusion: 时间覆盖长度是可调节的成本而非固定前提条件，性能退化是渐进且类别依赖的。这一结论为近实时制图和更快的土地覆盖更新周期提供了可行性证据。

Abstract: Many Earth Observation applications need land-use/land-cover maps that are both precise and frequently updated, yet the strongest Earth Observation foundation models build their embeddings from a full year of observations. We present a controlled study of the temporal sensitivity of Tessera, one of these leading foundation models, for land-use/land-cover mapping. Keeping the encoder frozen, we recompute its embeddings over varying observation windows, from a full year down to a single day. We use them as inputs to a linear probe and a UNet segmentation head, benchmarking both of them against from-scratch networks on LUCAS, DynamicEarthNet, and PASTIS-R datasets. We show that the value of the embeddings is task-dependent. Where classes are separated by phenology, as for the crop types of PASTIS-R, they reach a mean Intersection-over-Union of $58.3$, about $46\%$ above the best from-scratch model. Where classes are temporally stable (e.g., forests in DynamicEarthNet and LUCAS), embedding-based and from-scratch models match only under full supervision. On both datasets, Tessera embeddings remain markedly more label-efficient. Degradation under shorter temporal windows is gradual and class-dependent. Contracting the window from one year to one month costs $39\%$ of the segmentation accuracy on PASTIS-R but only $5\%$ on DynamicEarthNet. Single-day embeddings still classify land cover in LUCAS at $3.4$ times the chance level. Our study shows that temporal coverage is therefore a tunable cost rather than a fixed prerequisite, opening regimes such as near-real-time mapping and faster land-use/land-cover refresh cycles.

</details>


### [75] [Parameter-Efficient pretrained-CT-to-MRI Transfer for Rectal Cancer Segmentation: Performance-Calibration Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27178)
*Aneesh Rangnekar,Jorge Tapias Gomez,Joseph O Deasy,Harini Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SWIFT框架，利用在10444个CT体素上用DINOv2风格目标预训练的Swin V2编码器，通过四种配置（全微调、解码器压缩、LoRA、LoRA-解码器集成）对直肠癌MRI进行分割。结果显示SWIFTe（压缩配置）减少70.1%参数且提升肿瘤检出率，但存在检测率与边界一致性的权衡；SWIFTe-LDE4在校准误差上最优但绝对误差仍显示残余误校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直肠癌MRI分割对于自适应放疗和肿瘤反应评估至关重要，但实际部署需要兼顾计算效率和信息丰富的校准后不确定性估计。现有方法在准确性与效率、不确定性量化之间难以取得平衡，需要一个参数高效且具备校准能力的解决方案。

Method: 采用预训练的Swin V2编码器（在10444个公开3D CT体素上用DINOv2风格目标训练），将其适配到T2加权MRI，设计了四种累积配置：全微调（SWIFT）、解码器压缩（SWIFTe）、低秩适配（SWIFTe-LoRA）、四成员LoRA-解码器集成（SWIFTe-LDE4），在247例单中心保留测试集上进行几何精度、肿瘤检测、影像组学一致性和概率校准的多维度评估。

Result: SWIFTe相较于SWIFT减少70.1%参数（从72.8M降至21.8M），肿瘤检出率从89.9%提升至93.9%，中位表面DSC略降（0.61 vs 0.62），影像组学一致性改善。去除肿瘤感知增强使检出率从93.9%降至89.9%但表面DSC从0.61升至0.64，显示检测-边界权衡。SWIFTe-LoRA仅用14.6%的可训练参数保持相近性能。SWIFTe-LDE4经温度缩放后校准误差最低（ECE=0.217, Brier=0.222），但绝对ECE仍表明存在残余误校准。

Conclusion: 参数高效微调策略（特别是解码器压缩SWIFTe）在减少大量参数的同时可提升肿瘤检测率，LoRA-解码器集成在概率校准方面表现最佳，但所有配置的绝对校准误差仍不理想。效率-校准模式在公开VoCo检查点上得以复现，表明该模式对预训练初始化具有鲁棒性，但这反映的是跨初始化的稳健性而非跨机构临床泛化性。

Abstract: Accurate rectal cancer segmentation from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for adaptive radiotherapy and tumor response assessment, but deployment also requires computational efficiency and informative, calibrated uncertainty estimates. We therefore introduce SWIFT, a SWin pretrained model wIth parameter-eFficient and Tumor-aware fine-tuning for rectal cancer segmentation. A Swin V2 encoder pretrained on 10,444 public 3D CT volumes using a DINOv2-style objective was adapted to T2-weighted MRI through four cumulative configurations: full fine-tuning (SWIFT), decoder compression (SWIFTe), low-rank adaptation (SWIFTe-LoRA), and a four-member LoRA-decoder ensemble (SWIFTe-LDE4). Geometric accuracy, tumor detection, radiomic agreement, and probability calibration were evaluated on a held-out 247-case test set from a single-institution cohort acquired using 1.5 or 3 Tesla GE scanners. Compared with SWIFT, SWIFTe reduced total parameters by 70.1% (from 72.8M to 21.8M) and increased tumor detection rate from 89.9% to 93.9%, while achieving a slightly lower median surface DSC (0.61 versus 0.62) and improved radiomic agreement. In a separate SWIFTe ablation, removing tumor-aware augmentation reduced detection from 93.9% to 89.9% but increased surface DSC from 0.61 to 0.64, demonstrating a detection-boundary-agreement trade-off. SWIFTe-LoRA used 14.6% of SWIFTe's trainable parameters while retaining similar segmentation performance. SWIFTe-LDE4 achieved the lowest calibration errors among the four configurations after temperature scaling (expected calibration error, 0.217; Brier score, 0.222), although the absolute expected calibration error indicates residual miscalibration. Similar efficiency-calibration patterns were observed using the public VoCo checkpoint, supporting robustness across pretrained initializations rather than external clinical generalizability.

</details>


### [76] [SSMB: Self-Supervised Local Feature Detection under Motion Blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27181)
*Zhenjun Zhao,Fabio Bellavia,Wenting Wang,Fan Zhu,Jiajun Wu,Suryansh Kumar,Mingqiang Wei,Haoang Li,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SSMB是一种针对运动模糊图像的无需去模糊的自监督关键点检测器。它通过局部可判别性增强模块和两阶段训练（几何预训练+模糊感知训练），在关键点检测、图像匹配、相对位姿估计和视觉定位等任务上达到稀疏关键点检测器的最新水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动模糊会破坏局部图像结构并降低关键点定位的重复性。现有方法要么依赖计算昂贵的先去模糊再检测流程，可能引入恢复伪影；要么在清晰图像上回归手工设计的关键点位置，反映的是手工检测器的假设而非模糊下真正可重复的关键点。

Method: SSMB采用两阶段训练：（1）几何预训练阶段在合成形状上自举空间可判别性的关键点检测，无需外部检测器；（2）模糊感知训练阶段在真实清晰-模糊图像对上通过多组件自监督目标学习模糊不变性检测，该目标约束跨域一致性、几何对齐和空间覆盖。此外引入局部可判别性增强（LDE）模块，在全局特征混合后恢复细粒度局部判别性。

Result: 在运动模糊条件下对关键点检测、图像匹配、相对位姿估计和视觉定位的广泛评估表明，SSMB在稀疏关键点检测器中建立了新的最先进水平，在监督和自监督基线中均持续取得最优性能。

Conclusion: SSMB证明了无需去模糊和无需手工检测器即可实现模糊不变的自监督关键点检测，为运动模糊图像中的关键点检测提供了一种更高效可靠的方案，代码、模型和数据集将公开。

Abstract: Keypoint detection under motion blur remains a significant challenge, as blur distorts local image structure and degrades the repeatability of feature localization. Existing approaches either rely on computationally expensive deblur-then-detect pipelines that may introduce restoration artifacts, or learn to regress the image positions of handcrafted keypoints extracted on sharp images, which reflects the assumptions of the handcrafted detector rather than what is truly repeatable under blur. We present SSMB, a deblur-free, self-supervised keypoint detector for motion-blurred images that requires neither handcrafted detectors nor external pseudo-labels. SSMB introduces the Local Discriminability Enhancement (LDE) module, which restores fine-grained local discriminability after global feature mixing. Training is performed in two stages. First, geometric pretraining on synthetic shapes bootstraps spatially discriminative keypoint detection without any external detector, just from the rendered geometry. Second, blur-aware training on real sharp-blur image pairs learns blur-invariant detection through a multi-component self-supervised objective that enforces cross-domain consistency, geometric alignment, and spatial coverage. Extensive evaluations on keypoint detection, image matching, relative pose estimation, and visual localization under motion blur demonstrate that SSMB establishes a new state-of-the-art among sparse keypoint detectors, consistently outperforming both supervised and self-supervised baselines across all tasks. Code, models, and datasets will be publicly available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [77] [Unsupervised Adaptation of 3D CT Foundation Models for 3D CBCT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27190)
*Gauthier Miralles,Loic Le Folgoc,Vincent Jugnon,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于冗余减少特征对齐的无监督域适应（UDA）框架，用于解决锥形束CT（CBCT）3D分割中标注数据稀缺和与诊断CT域差异大的双重挑战。该框架与架构无关，可适配CNN和ViT基础模型，在介入血管手术和放射治疗两个CT-CBCT肝脏分割基准上优于现有预训练基础模型和UDA策略，并开源代码、模型权重及公开CBCT数据集的肝脏分割结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBCT的准确3D分割对介入治疗和放射治疗至关重要，但面临两大挑战：标注CBCT数据稀缺，以及介入CBCT与传统CT之间存在由采集方式、物理效应和造影剂特异性血管内容导致的显著模态差异，限制了跨模态模型迁移的有效性。

Method: 提出基于冗余减少特征对齐的UDA框架，在特征空间中显式弥合CT与CBCT之间的模态鸿沟，无需目标域标注或推理时自适应；该框架与架构无关，可同时适配CNN和ViT基础分割模型。

Result: 在两个具有挑战性的CT-CBCT肝脏分割基准（介入血管手术和放射治疗）上进行评估，结果表明即使大规模预训练分割网络也需要显式特征空间桥接才能跨采集模态泛化，所提方法在性能上持续优于现有预训练基础模型和UDA策略。

Conclusion: 冗余减少的特征对齐是实现无监督CT-CBCT跨模态分割的有效手段，所提框架可为CBCT分割提供无需标注的可行方案，并通过开源资源支持可复现性和未来基准测试。

Abstract: Accurate 3D segmentation of cone-beam CT (CBCT) is critical for interventional and radiation therapy applications, yet it remains limited by two compounding challenges: the scarcity of annotated CBCT data and the large domain shift from diagnostic CT. Interventional CBCT exhibits fundamental modality differences from conventional CT, driven by acquisition and physics effects as well as contrast-specific vascular content, thereby limiting effective cross-modality model transfer. We propose a novel unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) framework based on redundancy-reducing feature alignment, enabling 3D CBCT segmentation with no target-domain annotations or inference-time adaptation. Our framework is architecture-agnostic, seamlessly adapting both CNN-based and ViT-based foundation models. We evaluate our method on two challenging CT-CBCT liver segmentation benchmarks: one for interventional vascular procedures and one for radiation therapy, demonstrating that even large-scale pretrained segmentation networks require explicit feature-space bridging to generalize across acquisition modalities, and that our approach consistently outperforms existing pretrained foundation model and UDA strategies. To support reproducibility and benchmarking, we release the liver segmentations for a public CBCT dataset, along with the code, trained models, and weights.

</details>


### [78] [Vision-centric generative AI models: A software-hardware perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27199)
*Eleni Tselepi,Cristian Sestito,Shady Agwa,Themis Prodromakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇Perspective文章指出，视觉生成式AI的进展主要由输出质量驱动，硬件被动地响应模型需求的增长。文章量化了这些模型在各种加速平台上的参数成本和能效，将四类生成模型映射到七个真实应用领域，并倡导软件-硬件协同设计方法，从设计过程一开始就考虑部署约束，确保"正确的模型"在"正确的硬件"上为"正确的应用"服务，使生成式AI的部署在更广泛的平台上具有可持续性和可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成式AI虽然广泛应用于数据中心的大规模文本到图像应用，但其在边缘端（如自动驾驶、农业传感器、移动设备）也至关重要——这些场景受严格的硬件约束。然而，当前的进展重心在输出质量，硬件被动适应模型的增长需求，导致边缘部署面临可持续性和可及性挑战。

Method: 作者采用多维度分析框架：(1)量化多种加速器平台上视觉生成模型的参数成本和能量效率；(2)将四种生成模型家族（四类生成模型架构）映射到七个真实世界应用领域，以系统性地揭示模型需求与硬件能力之间的错配。

Result: 分析揭示了几代视觉生成模型参数和能耗的快速增长趋势，以及不同生成模型家族在不同应用领域中的适配性差异，展示了当前"质量优先"范式下的硬件追赶模式不可持续。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁从设计初期就引入部署约束的软件-硬件协同设计理念，以实现"正确模型-正确硬件-正确应用"的三者匹配，从而在更广泛的平台范围内实现生成式AI部署的可持续性与可及性。

Abstract: Vision generative artificial intelligence (AI) has emerged as one of the most rapidly advancing areas of deep learning. The explosion of multimodal models has made them widely associated with text-to-image applications running on large datacentres. However, vision generative models are equally needed in applications that operate under strict hardware constraints at the edge, including autonomous vehicles, agricultural sensors, and mobile devices. In this Perspective, we argue that progress in vision generative AI has been driven by output quality, with hardware evolving reactively to accommodate growing model demands. We quantify the parameter cost and energy efficiency of these models across a range of accelerator platforms, and map four generative model families against seven real-world application domains. Finally, we advocate a software-hardware co-design approach, where deployment constraints are considered from the start of the design process, ensuring that the "right model" runs on the "right hardware" to serve the "right application", making generative AI deployment sustainable and accessible across a much broader range of platforms.

</details>


### [79] [PACE: A Unified Condense-and-Extract Paradigm for Fast VLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27206)
*Junjie Liu,Shengyuan Ye,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE是一个无需训练的推理加速框架，通过统一的Condense-and-Extract范式同时优化视觉编码器和LLM阶段，在仅使用10%视觉token的情况下保留93.8%的性能，实现3.1倍的首token延迟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型中的视觉token数量激增导致推理成本急剧上升，而现有token剪枝方法存在两个根本性局限：1)大多数方法只在视觉编码器之后操作，无法优化视觉编码阶段的延迟；2)在严格token预算下难以同时保留整体视觉上下文和细粒度细节，导致性能下降。

Method: PACE框架包含两个核心组件：1)Condense阶段的自适应像素压缩器(APC)，在编码前评估视觉信息密度并自适应下采样冗余输入，减少编码器计算同时保留全局上下文和关键视觉线索；2)Extract阶段的动态双注意力提取器(DDAE)，通过融合编码器的内部视觉信号和LLM的语义信号选择性保留视觉token，保护任务关键细节。

Result: 将PACE集成到Qwen2.5-VL-7B后，模型在仅使用10%视觉token的情况下保留了93.8%的原始性能，首token延迟(TTFT)实现了3.1倍的加速。

Conclusion: PACE通过统一的两阶段范式有效解决了VLM推理中视觉token冗余问题，在不进行训练的情况下同时加速了视觉编码器和LLM两个阶段，证明了无需额外训练即可实现高效VLM推理的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate exceptional visual reasoning capabilities, yet their inference costs escalate rapidly with the proliferation of visual tokens. Existing visual token pruning methods exhibit two fundamental limitations. First, most approaches operate exclusively post-vision encoder, leaving the substantial latency of the visual encoding phase unoptimized. Second, under strict token budgets, these methods often fail to jointly preserve holistic visual contexts and fine-grained details, leading to performance degradation. To address these bottlenecks, we propose PACE (Pixel-Adaptive Condense and Extract), a training-free inference framework that accelerates both the vision encoder and the Large Language Model (LLM) via a unified Condense-and-Extract paradigm. During the Condense stage, an Adaptive Pixel Compressor (APC) evaluates visual information density prior to encoding, adaptively downsampling redundant inputs, curtailing encoder computation while preserving global context and essential visual cues. In the Extract stage, a Dynamic Dual-Attention Extractor (DDAE) selectively retains visual tokens via a fusion of internal visual signals from the encoder and semantic signals from the LLM, safeguarding task-critical details. By integrating PACE into Qwen2.5-VL-7B, the model retains 93.8% of its original performance while utilizing only 10% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.1x speedup in time to first token (TTFT). Our code is available at https://github.com/jjL357/PACE.

</details>


### [80] [CODE: Cross-Modal Calibration and Dynamic Suppression for Open World Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27214)
*Hao Xu,Zhaoning Shi,Hehe Jin,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CODE是一个用于开放世界目标检测的推理时统一框架，通过跨模态联合置信度校准、不确定性引导的通用目标增强和基于置信度裕度的动态异常抑制三个互补组件，解决多模态基础模型中的语义歧义和刚性异常惩罚问题，在Real-World Detection基准上超越现有最佳方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界目标检测（OWOD）基于多模态基础模型时，存在两个关键问题：(1) 单向文本到视觉匹配导致语义歧义；(2) 刚性异常值惩罚会过度抑制靠近已知类决策边界的未知对象。这两个问题限制了OWOD在实际场景中的检测性能。

Method: CODE提出三个互补的推理时组件：(1) 跨模态联合置信度校准（Cross-Modal Joint Confidence Calibration）——注入全局视觉原型来校准基于文本的已知类预测；(2) 不确定性引导的通用目标增强（Uncertainty-Guided Universal Objectness Enhancement）——通过局部视觉响应的分类不确定性来增强潜在未知对象；(3) 基于置信度裕度的动态异常抑制（Dynamic Outlier Suppression via Confidence Margin）——用裕度感知的调整替代刚性抑制，保留模糊的分布外实例。

Result: 在Real-World Detection基准上，使用OWL-ViT L/14骨干网络，CODE在Task 1上达到21.7 U-mAP和40.8 K-mAP，分别超越先前最先进方法2.6和2.3个百分点。

Conclusion: CODE通过推理时的模块化设计，在不重新训练的情况下有效缓解了多模态OWOD中的语义歧义和过抑制问题，验证了动态阈值和跨模态校准策略在开放世界检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Open World Object Detection (OWOD) built on multimodal foundation models often suffers from semantic ambiguity caused by unidirectional text-to-vision matching, while rigid outlier penalties may over-suppress unknown objects near known-class decision boundaries. We propose CODE (Cross-Modal Calibration and Dynamic Suppression), a unified inference-time framework with three complementary components. Cross-Modal Joint Confidence Calibration injects global visual prototypes to calibrate text-driven known-class predictions. Uncertainty-Guided Universal Objectness Enhancement measures classification hesitation from local visual responses to strengthen potential unknown objects. Dynamic Outlier Suppression via Confidence Margin replaces rigid suppression with a margin-aware adjustment that preserves ambiguous out-of-distribution instances. Experiments on the Real-World Detection benchmark demonstrate that, with the OWL-ViT L/14 backbone, CODE achieves 21.7 U-mAP and 40.8 K-mAP in Task 1, surpassing the previous state of the art by 2.6 and 2.3 points, respectively.

</details>


### [81] [DINOcular: Self-Supervised Visuospatial Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27226)
*Farkhat Almukhamedov,Sami Azirar,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自监督框架，从RGB-D观测中学习联合视觉-空间表征。该方法通过补丁间和补丁内融合将深度几何先验整合到视觉骨干网络中，使模型同时编码外观和空间结构，在3D几何基准上超越同等规模的方法，同时保持语义分割任务的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉基础模型几乎仅在RGB图像上训练，但许多具身系统能够获取显式深度传感信息，这提供了单目输入无法恢复的几何信息。现有方法未能有效利用深度信息来增强视觉表征的3D感知能力。

Method: 提出一个自监督框架，通过补丁间（inter-patch）和补丁内（intra-patch）融合机制，将由深度数据导出的几何先验与视觉骨干网络整合。该框架使模型能够高效编码外观特征和空间结构信息。

Result: 该表示在3D感知方面展现出显著提升，同时保持语义迁移能力：在多个3D几何基准上超越同等规模的前序方法，并在标准RGB-D语义分割任务上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过有效融合深度几何先验，自监督学习可以生成同时具备语义迁移性和3D空间感知能力的联合视觉-空间表征，为具身智能系统提供了更优的视觉基础表征。

Abstract: We introduce a self-supervised framework for learning joint visuospatial representations from RGB-D observations. While modern vision foundation models are trained almost exclusively on RGB images, many embodied systems have access to explicit depth sensing, which provides geometric information that monocular inputs cannot recover. Our method integrates depth-derived geometric priors with a visual backbone through inter-patch and intra-patch fusion, enabling the model to encode both appearance and spatial structure efficiently. The resulting representation shows promising improvements on 3D awareness while preserving semantic transfer: it outperforms prior methods of comparable scale on multiple 3D geometry benchmarks, and remains competitive when probed for standard RGB-D semantic segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [82] [UniFLM: United Segmentation and Measurement on Fetal Limb Ultrasonic Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27240)
*Zeen Zhou,Qiuhua Chen,Xiaojun Cao,Changmao Chen,Chao Sun,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了胎儿四肢长骨（FLB）数据集，包含肱骨、股骨、胫腓骨和桡尺骨的高质量标注，并提出了UniFLM框架，通过语义感知跳跃连接、正样本采样和点回归映射模块，实现跨切面胎儿长骨的自动分割与测量，在FLB数据集上优于现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前超声检查对胎儿肢体发育评估和先天畸形检测至关重要，但现有AI模型因缺乏高质量标注数据和统一框架而常忽略胎儿致死性骨骼发育不良；此外，通用分割模型难以应对超声图像中的固有噪声和语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出UniFLM统一框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）Semantic-Aware Skip Connection模块，弥合编码器与解码器特征间的语义鸿沟；（2）Positive Sampling策略，自适应过滤噪声并提取关键语义信息；（3）Point Regression Mapping模块，学习临床医生标注模式以实现精确的骨长测量。同时构建了FLB数据集，涵盖四种胎儿长骨的高质量标注。

Result: 在FLB数据集上的大量实验表明，UniFLM在胎儿长骨评估中相比当前最先进模型取得了更优的精度和更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniFLM为胎儿长骨超声图像分割与测量提供了一种统一且有效的解决方案，在精度和泛化性上均超越现有方法，有望推动产前骨骼发育异常的自动化筛查。

Abstract: Prenatal ultrasound examination is crucial for assessing fetal limb development and detecting congenital anomalies. However, existing artificial intelligence models often overlook fetal lethal skeletal dysplasias due to the lack of high-quality annotated data and a unified framework for multiple long bones. Moreover, generic segmentation models struggle with the inherent noise and semantic gaps in ultrasound images. To address these challenges, we construct the Fetal Limb Bones (FLB) dataset, comprising high-quality annotations for the humerus, femur, tibia-fibula, and radius-ulna. Furthermore, we propose UniFLM, a unified framework for automatic cross-plane segmentation and measurement. UniFLM incorporates a Semantic-Aware Skip Connection module to bridge the semantic gap between encoder and decoder features, and a Positive Sampling strategy to adaptively filter noise and extract essential semantic information. Finally, a Point Regression Mapping module is introduced to learn clinician annotation patterns for precise bone length measurement. Extensive experiments conducted on the FLB dataset demonstrate that the proposed UniFLM achieves superior accuracy and enhanced generalization capabilities in fetal long bone assessment compared to current state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [83] [Sidecar: Training-Free Semantic Reuse for Character-Consistent Free-form Visual Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27280)
*Sibo Dong,Sarah Adel Bargal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sidecar是一个即插即用的语义增强模块，用于解决自由形式故事生成中角色一致性难以维持的问题。它从首次出现的完整描述中保留实体级信息，并将其注入到后续不完整的提示嵌入中，无需额外训练或修改基础扩散模型架构。在FreeStoryBench上的实验表明，Sidecar在多个SDXL和FLUX基线模型上持续提升了提示-图像对齐和角色一致性，且计算开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自由形式的故事生成中，角色只在首次出现时被完整描述，后续仅通过类型级提及或代词来引用。这种设置更接近自然叙事，但后续提示往往缺失重要的身份相关语义，使得保持角色一致性变得困难。现有方法难以在此场景下维持一致的视觉身份。

Method: 提出Sidecar，一个即插即用的语义增强模块。它从角色的初始完整描述中保留实体级信息，并将缺失的语义注入到后续提示的嵌入中。该模块无需额外训练，也不修改基础扩散模型（如SDXL和FLUX）的架构。

Result: 在FreeStoryBench基准上的实验显示，Sidecar在多个SDXL和FLUX基线上持续改善了提示-图像对齐和角色一致性，同时引入的计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: Sidecar为自由形式故事生成中的角色一致性提供了一种轻量、通用的解决方案，无需训练或架构改动即可显著提升跨帧角色身份保持能力。

Abstract: Visual storytelling requires generating images that follow a narrative while preserving consistent character identities across frames. In free-form story generation, a character is fully described only when first introduced and is later referred to by a type-level mention or pronoun. Although this setting better reflects natural storytelling, later prompts may omit important identity-related semantics, making character consistency more difficult to maintain. We propose \textbf{Sidecar}, a plug-and-play semantic augmentation module that preserves entity-level information from the initial description and injects the missing semantics into later prompt embeddings. Sidecar requires no additional training and does not modify the architecture of the base diffusion model. Experiments on FreeStoryBench show that Sidecar consistently improves prompt-image alignment and character consistency across multiple SDXL- and FLUX-based baselines, with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [84] [TADP: Task-Aware Deformable Prediction for Single-Stage 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27282)
*Su Wang,Yaochen Li,Min Yang,Jiaohao Nie,Chang Liu,Yuehu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务感知变形预测（TADP）方法用于单阶段3D目标检测，通过三重特征细化聚合模块、多尺度特征聚合块和即插即用的任务感知变形头来解决不同检测任务特征空间不兼容的问题，在KITTI数据集上汽车mAP达到80.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数单阶段3D目标检测器使用相同的提取特征来完成不同任务（如分类、定位等），但将所有任务的特征投影到一个能够适应全部任务的公共空间是不可能的，这限制了检测性能。

Method: 1）设计三重特征细化聚合模块自适应地提取三层特征；2）设计多尺度特征聚合块以尺度感知方式融合多尺度特征；3）设计即插即用的任务感知变形头，能够感知各任务的重点和交互关系，并包含三种不同的变形模块。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上，汽车的mAP达到80.91%，超越了KITTI基准上的许多最先进方法；所提出的变形头在其他检测方法上也表现出良好的迁移效果。

Conclusion: 通过任务感知的变形预测机制，可以有效解决单阶段3D检测器中多任务特征空间冲突的问题，提升检测精度，且变形头具有良好的通用性和可移植性。

Abstract: Most single-stage 3D object detectors complete different tasks with the same extracted features. Nevertheless, it is impossible to project features into a common space that is adaptive for all the tasks. We present a novel task-aware deformable prediction (TADP) method for single-stage 3D object detection to solve this problem. Firstly, a triple feature refinement aggregation module is designed to extract three-level features adaptively. Additionally, we design the multi-scale feature aggregation block to fuse multi-scale features in a scale-aware manner. Finally, the prediction of each task is deformed with the designed plug-and-play task-aware deformation head. It can percept the emphasis and interaction of each task. We also designed three different deformation modules. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed deformation head shows good results on other detection methods. The experimental results on the KITTI dataset demonstrate that the car mAP is 80.91%, surpassing many state-of-the-art methods on the KITTI benchmark.

</details>


### [85] [Detection of Christmas tree plantations from high-resolution aerial imagery. A case study in the French Morvan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27290)
*Francesca Razzano,Emanuele Dalsasso,Adrien Baysse-Lainé,Silvia Liberata Ullo,Gilda Schirinzi,Jocelyn Chanussot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将圣诞树种植园制图定义为遥感中的罕见目标语义分割问题，提出了硬负样本挖掘（HNM）策略以改善对混淆背景的判别能力，并在监督测试、时间迁移和大规模验证三个层面进行评估。最佳模型（DeepLabV3+ResNet-34）在2020年测试集上达到IoU 0.733、F1 0.846，HNM将PR-AUC从0.204提升至0.913，并在2017/2018和2023年的时间迁移中展示了良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圣诞树种植园具有重要经济价值，但遥感领域对其研究几乎空白。其制图面临种植密度高、轮作周期短、与周围植被视觉混淆严重、仅有一年稠密标签以及景观尺度上的严重类别不平衡等多重挑战。现有深度学习方法主要针对森林、通用种植园或果园设计，未能显式处理圣诞树种植园的结构特异性和难负样本混淆问题。

Method: 本文提出三方面贡献：(i)将圣诞树种植园制图形式化为一个独立的罕见目标语义分割问题；(ii)引入硬负样本挖掘（HNM）策略，增强模型对混淆背景模式的判别力；(iii)在监督测试、时间迁移和大规模验证三个互补层面评估所提出的框架。模型采用DeepLabV3架构配备ResNet-34编码器，并结合HNM策略进行训练。

Result: 在2020年测试集上，最佳模型（DeepLabV3+ResNet-34）达到IoU 0.733和F1-score 0.846。HNM显著改善了精度-召回率行为，将PR曲线下面积从0.204提升至0.913。时间迁移实验显示良好的可迁移性，2017/2018年达到IoU/F1为0.751/0.858，2023年为0.691/0.817。大规模验证显示该任务的内在困难性——圣诞树种植园仅占总评估范围面积的极小部分：2017/2018年为1,498.4公顷（1.72%），2023年为1,782.2公顷（2.04%），总面积87,309.4公顷。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将圣诞树种植园作为独立罕见目标进行语义分割制图的可行性，HNM策略在类别极度不平衡和硬负样本混淆的条件下显著提升了模型性能。时间迁移实验表明模型具备跨年份泛化能力，而大规模验证揭示了该任务的固有难度，为后续研究提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: Christmas tree plantations are economically relevant, yet a largely unexplored application domain in Remote Sensing (RS). Their delineation is challenging because of high planting density, short rotation cycles, visual confusion with surrounding vegetation, the availability of dense labels for one reference year only, and severe class imbalance at the landscape scale. Although Deep Learning (DL) methods have shown strong potential for vegetation mapping, existing approaches are typically designed for forests, generic plantation systems, or orchards, and do not explicitly address the structural specificity and hard-negative confusion that characterize Christmas tree plantations. In response to these challenges, this work makes three main contributions: (i) it frames Christmas tree plantation mapping as a distinct rare-target semantic segmentation problem; (ii) it introduces a Hard Negative Mining (HNM) strategy to improve discrimination against confusing background patterns; and (iii) it evaluates the proposed framework across complementary levels, including supervised testing, temporal transfer, and large-scale validation. On the 2020 test set held out, the best model, DeepLabV3 with a ResNet-34 encoder, achieves an IoU of 0.733 and an F1-score of 0.846. HNM substantially improves precision-recall behavior, increasing the area under the precision-recall curve from 0.204 to 0.913. Temporal inference further shows meaningful transferability, reaching IoU/F1 values of 0.751/0.858 on 2017/2018 and 0.691/0.817 on 2023. Large-scale validation further highlights the intrinsic difficulty of the task, as Christmas tree plantations occupied only a very small fraction of the extent of the common evaluation, corresponding to 1,498.4 ha (1.72\%) in 2017/2018 and 1,782.2 ha (2.04\%) in 2023 out of 87,309.4 ha in total.

</details>


### [86] [R2M-Bench: Evaluating Revisit Memory via Relative Consistency in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27328)
*Qiwen Gu,Bingjie Gao,Rui Chen,Geng Li,Jifan Li,Qishuai Wen,Li Niu,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Junqiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R2M-Bench基准，通过同一条rollout内的相对校准（将回访帧对与gap匹配的非回访对及短程对对比）来区分视频世界模型的"回访记忆一致性"与"通用时间稳定性"，解决了绝对回访分数易受渲染稳定性、重复内容和运动失败影响的歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型的回访记忆评估仅对比首次访问帧与返回帧的绝对相似度，但这无法证明模型真正记住了场景——因为rollout变化很小、内容重复或运动失败都可能产生高相似度，导致评估结果具有歧义。

Method: R2M-Bench对每个检测到的回访对，在相同rollout中引入两类对照：gap匹配的非回访对（衡量通用时间稳定性）和短程对（估计短视野一致性）。据此计算MemoryGain（MG，回访相对时间基线的优势）和Normalized Memory Ratio（NMR，将优势按短程到基线的动态范围归一化）。基准包含100个参考场景×3条离开-返回轨迹共300个实例，评估外观保真度、场景与物体身份、局部几何和持久状态。

Result: 在7个动作条件视频世界模型上，Overall NMR与人类一致性判断的Spearman相关系数为ρ=0.547（95% CI [0.45,0.63]）；其与生成运动的模型内相关为0.072，远低于原始回访相似度的0.207，表明相对校准显著减少了"慢动作捷径"。DreamX-World-Memo在Overall NMR上取得最高分。

Conclusion: 同rollout内的相对校准是一种实用且有效的方式，能够将回访特异的记忆一致性从通用时间稳定性中分离出来，为视频世界模型的记忆评估提供了更可靠的度量标准。

Abstract: High similarity between first-visit and return frames does not necessarily show that a video world model remembered the scene; the intervening rollout may simply have changed very little. This ambiguity makes absolute revisit scores sensitive to rendering stability, repetitive content, and failed motion. We introduce \emph{R2M-Bench} (\textbf{R}elative \textbf{R}evisit \textbf{M}emory Benchmark), a benchmark of observable revisit-selective consistency. For every detected return, R2M-Bench compares the revisit pair with two controls from the same rollout: a gap-matched non-revisit pair that measures generic temporal stability and a short-range pair that estimates short-horizon consistency. These comparisons produce \emph{MemoryGain} (MG), the revisit advantage over the temporal baseline, and the \emph{Normalized Memory Ratio} (NMR), which normalizes this advantage by the short-to-baseline dynamic range. R2M-Bench combines 100 reference scenes with three leave-and-return trajectories to form 300 instances and evaluates appearance fidelity, scene and object identity, local geometry, and persistent state. Across seven action-conditioned video world models, Overall NMR correlates with human consistency judgments at Spearman's $ρ=0.547$ (95\% CI $[0.45,0.63]$). Its within-model correlation magnitude with generated motion is $0.072$, compared with $0.207$ for raw revisit similarity, indicating that relative calibration substantially reduces the slow-motion shortcut. DreamX-World-Memo achieves the highest Overall NMR among the evaluated video models. Together, these results support same-rollout relative calibration as a practical way to distinguish revisit-specific consistency from generic temporal stability.

</details>


### [87] [PAWBench: How Far Are We from Probabilistically Aligned World Modeling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27345)
*Yuandong Pu,Le Zhuo,Sayak Paul,Gabriel Jorge Menezes,Avram Đorđević,Shiyang Li,Yifan Zhou,Bin Fu,Wenlong Zhang,Junjun He,Yu Qiao,Yihao Liu,Jingbo Xing,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视频生成模型作为世界模型时应满足"概率对齐"这一分布级标准，并构建了PAWBench基准和PAWEval评估协议。在50个场景和11个系统上的实验表明，当前视频生成模型均无法同时匹配参考概率分布并覆盖有效行为范围，且语言提示、噪声采样和训练都无法有效重塑模型的预测分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型评估主要关注单个视频的合理性，忽略了世界模型应复现同一初始观测和动作下可能行为的分布这一关键要求。作者提出概率对齐概念，旨在填补这一评估空白，量化当前视频生成器与世界模型之间的差距。

Method: 1) 形式化定义概率对齐作为世界模型的分布级标准；2) 构建PAWBench基准，包含50个多模态物理行为场景；3) 提出PAWEval结果级评估协议，将重复视频rollout转换为可能物理行为的经验分布；4) 在11个当前视频生成系统上进行系统评估，并测试语言提示、初始噪声采样和模型训练三种手段对预测分布的影响。

Result: 在50个场景和11个系统中，没有任何模型能同时匹配参考概率分布并覆盖有效行为的完整范围，表明当前视频生成模型在概率对齐方面存在系统性缺口。语言提示、初始噪声采样和模型训练三种干预手段均未能有效重塑模型的预测分布。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成器尚未实现概率对齐的世界建模，该工作为未来向概率对齐世界模型方向发展提供了基准和评估基础。

Abstract: Recent video generation models are increasingly framed as world models. Many physical processes can unfold in more than one valid way. Therefore, a world model should reproduce not only a plausible trajectory, but also the distribution of possible behaviors under the same initial observation and action. We call this distribution-level requirement probabilistic alignment. However, existing evaluations largely assess individual-video plausibility and do not test whether repeated generations recover the correct distribution. This raises a central question: how far are current video generators from probabilistically aligned world modeling? To answer it, we formalize probabilistic alignment as a distributional criterion for world models and introduce PAWBench, a benchmark for evaluating video generators as stochastic samplers of world dynamics. We further introduce PAWEval, an outcome-level protocol that converts repeated video rollouts into empirical distributions over possible physical behaviors. Across 50 scenarios and eleven current systems, no model consistently matches the reference probabilities while recovering the range of valid behaviors. Having established this gap, we test whether language prompts, initial noise sampling, or model training can reshape the model's predictive distribution. We believe our work can serve as a foundation for future efforts to move towards probabilistically aligned world modeling.

</details>


### [88] [KnockGS:interaction-Grounded Calibrationof Physical Gaussian Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27365)
*Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Bowen Jing,Jiyuan Cai,Xiaofeng Wang,Hongsen Lei,Weitao Zhou,Dandan Zhang,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KnockGS 是一个交互响应式物理高斯框架，通过从物体在已知外力下的动力学中提取时间响应特征来估算 3D 高斯弹性模量和密度尺度，并将估计结果冻结写回模拟器，使其能够预测未经过拟合的交互响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理集成 3D 高斯框架假设材料参数已知或需人工指定，限制了其在参数需要从观测动态中推断时的适用性。实际应用中材料参数往往未知，需要从物体动力学中自动标定。

Method: KnockGS 将力引起的响应转化为标定信号：从观测动力学中提取时间响应特征，据此估计弹性模量和密度尺度两个材料参数，然后将估计值冻结并写回同一模拟器中，用于在未拟合过的交互场景中测试。这是将物理模拟从纯前向过程转变为可逆标定过程的关键设计。

Result: 在五个保留材料的测试目标上，KnockGS 的参数恢复准确性显著优于响应检索、全局回归和固定默认材料方法；使用3D粒子轨迹、响应曲线统计和渲染帧质量评估表明，冻结的参数估计在方向和幅度均不同的交互下仍保持预测能力。

Conclusion: 交互响应中包含足够的信息来标定物理接地 3D 高斯表征中的材料尺度，这是迈向交互式物理高斯系统的重要第一步，使高斯资产的渲染外观与模拟响应保持一致。

Abstract: Physics-integrated 3D Gaussian representations now allow reconstructed deformable objects to be simulated and rendered under explicit material models. Existing pipelines, however, assume that material parameters are known or manually specified, limiting their applicability when these parameters must be inferred from observed object dynamics. We propose KnockGS, an interaction-response PhysicalGS framework that estimates the elasticity and density scales of a 3D Gaussian object from its dynamics under a known applied force. Rather than treating physical simulation only as a forward process, we turn the force-induced response into a calibration signal: temporal response features are xtracted from the observed dynamics, the two material scales are estimated from those features, and the estimate is then frozen and written back into the same simulator so that it can be tested on an interaction it was never fitted to.We evaluate the framework on both parameter recovery and response-level fidelity. The estimated scales are compared against hidden ground truth, and the re-simulated object is measured against the target using 3D particle trajectories, response-curve statistics, and rendered-frame quality. Across five held-out material targets, our method recovers the scales substantially more accurately than response retrieval, global regression, or a fixed default material, and the frozen estimate remains predictive under interactions that differ in direction and in magnitude. Interaction response therefore carries enough information to calibrate material scales in physically grounded 3D Gaussian representations.Our study is a first step toward interactive PhysicalGS systems that calibrate a Gaussian asset whose rendered appearance and simulated response are consistent.

</details>


### [89] [Successive Capacity Growth: Task-Complexity-Driven Width and Depth Expansion for Vision Transformer Encoders in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27367)
*Frederik Berenz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCG（Successive Capacity Growth）方法，使 JEPA 世界模型的编码器从极小的初始架构（1头、2层、283K参数）开始，通过函数保持扩展（function-preserving expansion）在宽度（注意力头）或深度（Transformer块）方向上按需逐级增长，由任务无关的测试-验证机制驱动，配合 SIGReg 正则化防止表示塌缩，在多个环境上实现显著优于固定大小编码器的参数效率和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 世界模型通常使用固定大小的 Vision Transformer 编码器，这类编码器对简单任务过度配置、对复杂任务配置不足，且注意力头之间存在显著冗余。作者希望打破"预分配最大容量"的范式，让编码器容量随任务复杂度自适应增长，从而提升参数和计算效率。

Method: 1) SCG：从最小编码器出发，通过函数保持扩展在宽度（增加注意力头以捕捉低层语义容量）或深度（增加Transformer块以提升高阶语义抽象）方向逐级生长；2) 任务无关的测试-验证机制：安全试运行架构变更，若预测损失未改善则回滚；3) SIGReg（Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer）：确保所有语义维度统计独立且与预测目标对齐，防止表示塌缩。

Result: 在60维多目标动力学任务上，SCG 自然触发深度扩展，预测损失比固定小基线改善20.3%，参数效率比扩展到固定大模型高56倍；在2D导航任务上，单次宽度扩展比固定大模型改善23%。在全部三个环境上，自适应编码器匹配或超过固定小基线，零误报扩展，位精确函数保持（比率=1.0，绝对差=0.0）。

Conclusion: JEPA 世界模型的编码器无需预先分配最大容量，可以按任务需求逐级增长，在保持表示质量的同时实现显著的算力和数据效率提升。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) for world modeling typically employ fixed-size Vision Transformer encoders that are over-provisioned for simple tasks and under-provisioned for complex ones, with significant redundancy across attention heads. We propose Successive Capacity Growth (SCG), a method that starts from a minimal encoder (1 head, 2 layers, 283K parameters) and grows incrementally in width (adding attention heads for low-level semantic capacity) or depth (adding transformer blocks for higher-order semantic abstraction), driven by a task-agnostic test-and-verify mechanism that exploits function-preserving expansion to safely trial architectural changes and roll back if they do not improve prediction loss. The Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) ensures that all learned semantic dimensions remain statistically independent and aligned with the predictive objective, preventing collapse even as the architecture grows. On a 60-dimensional multi-object dynamics task, SCG naturally triggers depth expansion, improving prediction loss by 20.3% over the fixed small baseline with 56 times greater parameter efficiency than scaling to the fixed large model; on a 2D navigation task, a single width expansion yields even an 23% improvement over the fixed large model. Across all three tested environments of increasing complexity, the adaptive encoder matches or exceeds the fixed small baseline, with zero false-positive expansions and bit-exact function preservation (ratio = 1.0, absolute difference = 0.0). The take-away is that JEPA world model encoders need not be pre-allocated at maximum capacity - they can grow successively as the task demands, achieving significant compute and data efficiency while maintaining representation quality.

</details>


### [90] [LeVJEPA: Efficient & Scalable Video Pretraining without the Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27395)
*Lukas Kuhn,Lucas Maes,Giuseppe Serra,Quentin Le Lidec,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero,Florian Buettner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LeVJEPA，首个在LeJEPA无崩溃目标下训练的纯视频编码器。它摒弃了传统的动量目标编码器、停止梯度和掩码重建，仅用编码器+投影器和一个超参数，通过SIGReg正则化的不变性损失实现表征学习。训练成本由编码器观察的token数决定，随机token dropout在降低计算量的同时提升精度。在相同数据匹配epoch下，LeVJEPA以5.6-20.8倍更少的预训练计算达到或超越V-JEPA 2；在匹配FLOPs下，ImageNet-1K上超越最强视频基线7.6点。使用块因果注意力无精度损失，时间顺序成为编码器自身属性。实验表明移除计算开销后视频成为通用视觉预训练更优的基石。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频包含物理世界的时间结构，但自监督视频表征学习计算成本高昂。现有方法要么依赖架构不对称（动量目标编码器、停止梯度、容量受限预测器）来防止表征崩溃，要么通过像素空间重建掩码内容来规避崩溃，两者均造成显著计算开销。作者旨在探索一种去除这些机制后，视频是否能成为可行且更优的通用视觉预训练基石的命题。

Method: LeVJEPA采用LeJEPA的无崩溃目标，仅用单编码器和投影器构成，通过全局和局部视图上的不变性损失训练，并用SIGReg正则化以可证明保证排除表征崩溃，目标仅含单一超参数。架构无需分支间不对称，可使用块因果注意力。预训练成本由编码器观察的token数决定，通过均匀随机token dropping大幅降低计算量。

Result: 在相同数据匹配epoch下，LeVJEPA以5.6至20.8倍更少的预训练算力匹配或超越V-JEPA 2（ViT-S/B/L）。在匹配总FLOPs下，ImageNet-1K上超越最强视频基线7.6点，在运动中心基准上保持竞争力。块因果注意力下无精度损失。与计算匹配的帧级DINOv2相比，LeVJEPA在外观中心评估上接近图像预训练编码器，运动中心精度几乎翻倍。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，一旦移除计算开销瓶颈，视频凭借其时间结构成为通用视觉预训练中可行且在多个方面优于静态图像的基石。LeVJEPA的成功表明，无崩溃目标与token级计算效率的结合，使视频预训练不仅在计算上可负担，而且在外观与运动表征的通用性上具有独特优势。

Abstract: Video carries the temporal structure of the physical world, yet learning representations from it has remained computationally expensive: prevailing self-supervised methods either prevent representation collapse through architectural asymmetries, coupling an exponential-moving-average target encoder, a stop-gradient, and a capacity-limited predictor, or circumvent it by reconstructing masked content in pixel space. We introduce LeVJEPA, the first video encoder trained under LeJEPA's collapse-free objective, which dispenses with both. A single encoder is trained with an invariance loss over global and local views of a clip, regularized by SIGReg, which excludes collapse with a provable guarantee. The architecture reduces to an encoder and a projector, and the objective to a single hyperparameter. This formulation admits two properties. First, the cost of pretraining is governed by the number of tokens the encoder observes; uniform random token dropping renders this number small while simultaneously improving downstream accuracy. At matched epochs on identical data, LeVJEPA matches or surpasses V-JEPA 2 across ViT-S/B/L at 5.6 to 20.8x less pretraining compute, and at matched total FLOPs it exceeds the strongest video baseline by 7.6 points on ImageNet-1K while remaining competitive on motion-centric benchmarks. Second, since no asymmetry between branches is required, the encoder can be trained with block-causal attention at no measurable accuracy cost: temporal ordering becomes a property of the encoder itself. Against a compute-matched DINOv2 trained on frames of the same videos, LeVJEPA approaches the image-pretrained encoder on appearance-centric evaluation while nearly doubling its motion-centric accuracy. These results indicate that, once its computational overhead is removed, video becomes a viable and in several respects preferable substrate for general-purpose visual pretraining.

</details>


### [91] [Reconstructing Humans and Objects in Interaction using Large Reconstruction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27407)
*Agniv Chatterjee,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MILO是一个利用大型重建模型（LRM）的视觉能力从单张图像恢复详细3D人-物交互（3D HOI）的框架。其核心洞察是LRM提供了保留人与物体相对布局和接近线索的几何脚手架，将重建问题转化为解释LRM网格：分割为人体和物体组件，然后分别拟合参数化人体模型和可选的对象模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D人-物交互估计在AR/VR、机器人和具身AI中有广泛应用，但由于深度模糊性、遮挡和物体形状多样性而充满挑战。现有方法主要依赖重投影和接触约束，将参数化人体模型和物体模板拟合到2D图像上，未充分利用3D几何先验。

Method: MILO框架的关键创新在于将问题重新定义：首先利用大型重建模型（LRM）从单张图像生成保留人体-物体相对布局和接近线索的3D几何网格；然后将LRM网格分割为人体和物体两个组件；对人体部分拟合参数化身体模型；可选地，如果有物体模板，则将其与物体部分对齐。

Result: MILO在多个基准和交互场景上取得了强大的重建精度，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: LRM提供的几何脚手架能够有效简化和改进3D人-物交互重建，代码已开源。

Abstract: Estimation of Human-Object Interactions in 3D (3D HOI) is a fundamental problem in 3D computer vision with applications in AR/VR, robotics, and embodied AI. However, reconstructing these interactions in 3D remains challenging due to depth ambiguities, occlusions, and object shape variability. Existing approaches are primarily concerned with reprojection and contact constraints, fitting parametric human models and object templates to 2D images. In this paper, we explore a different avenue. We present MILO, a framework that leverages the visual capabilities of Large Reconstruction Models (LRMs) to recover detailed 3D human-object interactions from a single image. Our key observation is that LRMs provide a powerful geometric scaffold that preserves relative human-object arrangement and proximity cues. This significantly simplifies the reconstruction procedure, reframing the problem as interpreting the LRM mesh: we segment it into human and object components, fit a parametric body model to the human part, and optionally align an object template to the object part (if such a template is available). MILO achieves strong reconstruction accuracy and outperforms existing baselines across multiple benchmarks and interaction scenarios. Our code is available at https://ac5113.github.io/MILO.

</details>


### [92] [Retrieval Heads Meet Vision: Uncovering How VLMs Locate and Extract Visual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27417)
*Chanho Park,Daehyeon Choi,Jihyun Lee,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出"视觉检索头"(Visual Retrieval Heads, VRHs)概念，在视觉语言模型(VLM)中发现了一小部分注意力头（约1.7-2.6%）因果性地负责将文本描述与图像区域进行关联。通过统一设计空间对现有头部评分方法进行重构，作者发现从输出预测token对真实参照区域求和最能识别因果头。在11个VLM和5个指代表达基准上，仅屏蔽前20个VRH即可使定位精度下降高达80个百分点，而随机屏蔽同样数量的头几乎没有影响。VRHs还展现出跨任务泛化、功能特异性和跨架构共享性等新特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLM已在图像区域定位任务上表现出色（根据文本提示定位图像区域），但其内部机制一直未被理解。受大语言模型中"检索头"概念的启发，作者希望探究VLM是否也存在类似的用于视觉检索的因果机制，从而深入理解VLM如何将文本与视觉证据关联起来。

Method: 首先将现有的头部评分方法统一到一个设计空间中，涵盖查询token选择、键汇聚和跨样本汇聚三个维度。通过系统性比较，发现从输出预测token出发、对真实参照(ground-truth)区域进行求和最能可靠识别因果头。识别出VRHs后在11个VLM和5个数据集上进行因果验证实验，包括屏蔽头部测试、跨任务泛化测试、功能特异性分析和跨架构迁移测试。

Result: 发现VRHs约占注意力头的1.7-2.6%。屏蔽前20个VRH可使定位精度下降高达80个百分点，而随机屏蔽几乎无影响。VRHs具有三个新特性：(1)跨视觉参考任务泛化（对属性、空间、计数和视觉数学基准仍保持因果性）；(2)功能特异性（破坏定位但不影响输出格式）；(3)架构共享性（在共享同一LLM主干但视觉编码器、投影器和指令微调不同的VLM间可因果迁移）。

Conclusion: VLMs中存在与大语言模型检索头机制类似的视觉检索头，这验证了因果-稀疏-普适(causal-sparse-universal)三要素在视觉领域同样成立。此外，VRHs的跨任务泛化、功能特异性和跨架构共享性为理解VLM的视觉语言对应机制提供了新的视角，对模型可解释性和高效干预具有重要价值。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can locate an image region referred to by a text prompt and route the corresponding visual evidence to the output, yet the internal mechanism behind this behavior is not understood. Inspired by retrieval heads in large language models, we ask whether VLMs contain an analogous mechanism for visual retrieval. We answer affirmatively by introducing Visual Retrieval Heads (VRHs), a small subset of attention heads (about 1.7-2.6%) that are causally responsible for grounding text descriptions to image regions. To find them, we recast existing head-scoring methods under a unified design space over query tokens, key aggregation, and cross-sample aggregation. We then show that scoring attention from output prediction tokens with a sum over the ground-truth referent region most reliably identifies causal heads. Across eleven VLMs and five referring-expression benchmarks, masking only the top 20 VRHs reduces grounding accuracy by up to 80 percentage points, while masking the same number of random heads has little effect. Beyond replicating the causal-sparse-universal triad established for text retrieval heads, VRHs exhibit several properties not previously reported: they generalize across visual reference tasks, remaining causal on attribute, spatial, counting, and visual-math benchmarks despite being discovered through bounding-box prediction; they are functionally specific, preserving output format while corrupting localization; and they are architecturally shared, transferring causally across VLMs that share an LLM backbone but differ in vision encoder, projector, and instruction tuning.

</details>


### [93] [UrbanGround: From Local Perception to Spatial Agency in a Real-Scale City](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27456)
*Tianjie Ju,Zheng Wu,Yueqing Sun,Yuhan Cui,Bobo Li,Shengqiong Wu,Pengzhou Cheng,Haodong Zhao,Zongru Wu,Xinbei Ma,Doris Zhang,Kunling Li,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu,Hao Fei,Qi Gu,Gongshen Liu,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UrbanGround，一个基于香港真实3D地理空间数据构建的物理约束沙盒，用于测试多模态大语言模型（MLLM）代理能否将局部城市感知转化为可靠行动。通过三个递进的研究问题，发现当代MLLM代理在视觉识别和短程空间推理上表现良好，但在方向感和行人感知运动方面不可靠，且核心失败出现在扩展探索中——局部能力无法组合为持续的目标导向行为，错误不断累积且缺乏有效纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM能够解读街景，但城市能动性（urban agency）取决于这类局部证据在代理开始移动后是否仍然有用。当前缺乏一个物理约束的现实规模城市测试环境，无法系统地探究MLLM代理能将多少局部城市感知转化为可靠行动。

Method: 搭建UrbanGround沙盒，利用香港全域3D地理空间数据构建物理约束的城市副本，支持第一人称视角的闭环交互并提供交互式地图导航。通过三个递进的研究问题分析：1) 主动观察后能否锚定局部场景回答空间问题；2) 当地标变远且更不明确时该锚定能否支撑导航；3) 行为在路线可用性和行人运动变化下是否依然稳健。

Result: 当代MLLM代理在视觉识别和短程空间推理等原子能力上表现有用，但方向感和行人感知运动不可靠。核心失败出现在扩展探索中：局部能力无法组合成持续的目标导向行为，错误随探索累积且缺少有效纠正机制。

Conclusion: UrbanGround为研究当前MLLM代理在复杂、开放城市环境中能多可靠地进行探索提供了测试平台，期望支持更广泛的相关研究。研究揭示当前MLLM在局部能力向全局行为组合方面存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can interpret a street view, but urban agency depends on whether such local evidence remains useful after the agent starts to move. In this paper, we investigate how far current MLLM agents can turn local urban perception into reliable action in a complicated real-scale city. We propose UrbanGround, the first sandbox to make this question testable in a physically constrained replica of Hong Kong built from territory-wide 3D geospatial data. UrbanGround supports closed-loop interaction from a first-person view and provides an interactive map for navigation. Agents can directly enter the 3D city and explore from a first-person view. Our analysis follows the growth of the spatial problem through three research questions. We first test whether an agent can ground a local scene well enough to answer spatial questions after active observation. Then we ask whether that grounding supports navigation as destinations become farther away and less explicit. Finally, we examine whether the resulting behavior survives changes in route availability and pedestrian motion. Contemporary MLLM agents usually show useful atomic abilities in visual recognition and short-range spatial reasoning, while orientation and pedestrian-aware movement remain unreliable. Their central failure emerges over extended exploration, where local abilities do not compose into sustained goal-directed behavior and errors accumulate without effective correction. We hope UrbanGround will support broader study of how far current MLLM agents can explore reliably in complex, open-ended urban environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [TreeGraft: Adaptive Multi-Drafter Grafting for Tree-Based Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26112)
*Jiaming Fan,Daming Cao,Canchen Huang,Jiale Fu,Jin Zhang,Junjie Gao,Kai Yang,Xiangzhong Luo,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TreeGraft是一个多草稿器（multi-drafter）投机解码框架，通过让不同成本的草稿器联合构建共享草稿树来加速大语言模型推理。它利用更强草稿器对弱草稿器的候选进行重评分、重新选择嫁接位置并恢复被遗漏的路径，同时非破坏性地整合强草稿器扩展，并通过从离线价值系统中蒸馏出的轻量调度器控制调用强草稿器的时机。在10个模型对和6个基准上，TreeGraft相比两种固定单草稿器端点策略的较优者平均提升15.1%，最大提升26.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有树结构投机解码方法在所有起草步骤中使用单一草稿器，面临两难困境：较小的草稿器速度快但生成树质量低，较大的草稿器树质量高但延迟高。目标是打破这一权衡，同时获得小草稿器的速度和大草稿器的质量优势。

Method: TreeGraft提出多草稿器联合构建共享草稿树的框架。具体包括：(1) 用强草稿器对弱草稿器的候选进行重评分（rescoring）；(2) 重新选择嫁接位置（reselection）；(3) 恢复未被探索但有前景的路径；(4) 以非破坏性方式整合强草稿器扩展，保留仍可能被目标模型接受的已有分支；(5) 引入从离线价值系统蒸馏的轻量调度器来决定何时调用强草稿器以控制成本。

Result: 在10个模型对（模型组合）和6个基准上，TreeGraft优于两种固定单草稿器端点策略中的较优者，平均提升15.1%，最大提升达26.6%。

Conclusion: TreeGraft验证了多草稿器协作框架的有效性，通过动态调度和共享草稿树的方式有效解决了单一草稿器在速度与质量之间的权衡问题，为投机解码提供了新的设计方向。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference through a draft-then-verify paradigm. Building on this, tree-structured methods improve inference by organizing proposals into multiple candidate paths, increasing the accepted length. However, existing tree-structured methods use a single drafter for all drafting steps, creating a dilemma: a smaller drafter is fast but yields lower-quality trees, whereas a larger drafter improves tree quality but suffers from high latency. To address this, we propose TreeGraft, a multi-drafter framework in which drafters of different costs jointly construct a shared draft tree. TreeGraft uses the stronger drafter to rescore candidates by updating scores assigned by the weaker drafter, reselect grafting positions, and recover promising paths left unexplored. It also integrates stronger drafter expansions non-destructively, preserving existing branches that may still be accepted by the target model. Together, these designs improve the quality of the shared draft tree. To control the drafting cost, TreeGraft introduces a lightweight scheduler distilled from an offline value system to decide when to call the stronger drafter. Across 10 model pairs and 6 benchmarks, TreeGraft outperforms the better of the two fixed single-drafter endpoint strategies by 15.1% on average, reaching a maximum gain of 26.6%. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TreeGraft-E983.

</details>


### [95] [ElementCheck: Complexity-Aware Long-Form Text Factuality Evaluation via Sentence Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26118)
*Xinming Wang,Haoran Du,Yi Chen,Jian Xu,Hongming Yang,Han Hu,Yulong Chen,Cheng-Lin Liu,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ElementCheck，一种复杂度感知的长篇事实性验证框架，通过提取句子中的实体对作为元素并构建元素图来估计句子复杂度，实现简单句直接验证和复杂句的细粒度元素级验证，在FastFact-Sent及两个领域基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长篇事实性评估依赖于"分解-检索-验证"流水线，但该流水线存在两个关键问题：一是声明分解过程中的噪声，二是固定的验证粒度会导致不可靠的结果。这些问题使得长文本输出的事实性验证不够准确和可靠。

Method: ElementCheck框架的核心方法包括：(1) 不将句子均匀分解为原子子声明，而是提取原始句子中通过可验证连接显式关联的实体对作为"元素"；(2) 将这些元素组织成元素图，利用图的拓扑结构作为句子复杂度的结构信号；(3) 对简单句子进行直接验证，对复杂句子进行针对性的元素级细化与验证；(4) 构建新基准FastFact-Sent，将FastFact-Bench中的孤立声明映射回源句子以支持细粒度评估。

Result: 在FastFact-Sent和两个领域特定基准上的实验表明，ElementCheck在5个骨干模型上持续提升了事实性验证效果，同时保持了良好的准确率-成本权衡。进一步分析表明，复杂度感知验证减少了不必要的重新验证，并在不同骨干模型间保持稳定性。

Conclusion: ElementCheck通过复杂度感知的跨粒度验证策略，有效解决了传统流水线中声明分解噪声和固定验证粒度的问题，在提升事实性验证准确性的同时控制了计算成本，为长文本事实性评估提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Existing long-form factuality evaluation relies on the decompose-retrieve-verify pipeline. However, the pipeline suffers from noise from claim decomposition and fixed verification granularity, resulting in unreliable results. We propose ElementCheck, a complexity-aware framework that verifies long-form outputs via sentence elements. Instead of uniformly decomposing sentences into atomic sub-claims, ElementCheck extracts entity pairs that are explicitly linked through verifiable connections in the original sentence as elements, and organizes these into an element graph. The graph topology provides a structural signal for estimating sentence complexity, enabling direct verification for simple sentences and targeted element-level refinement and verification for complex ones. To support fine-grained evaluation, we construct a new benchmark FastFact-Sent by mapping isolated claims from FastFact-Bench back to their source sentences. Experiments on FastFact-Sent and two domain-specific benchmarks show ElementCheck consistently improves factuality verification across five backbone models while maintaining a favorable accuracy-cost trade-off. Further analyses demonstrate that complexity-aware verification reduces unnecessary re-verification and maintains stability across different backbones.

</details>


### [96] [DeflectBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Rhetorical Fallacy Generation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26119)
*Art Kanke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeflectBench基准，评估四个前沿LLM在23,990个生成样本上的修辞谬误生成行为，覆盖三种偏转策略、七种提示框架和80条主张。研究发现拒绝主要受请求结构而非主张内容影响；教育辩论教练提示可将拒绝率降至接近零；模型通常产生"标记合规"而非干净违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究聚焦于检测已有文本中的逻辑谬误，而较少关注LLM是否可被提示按需生成修辞谬误，以及当前安全后训练能否约束这种行为。作者旨在填补这一研究空白，系统评估前沿模型对谬误生成请求的响应行为。

Method: 构建DeflectBench评估框架，从四个前沿模型上收集23,990个生成样本，跨三种偏转策略（whataboutism、ad hominem、red herring）、七种提示框架和覆盖四个争议级别的80条主张进行系统性评估。分析拒绝行为、标记合规、软拒绝和干净合规四类响应分布及其影响因素。

Result: 拒绝主要由请求结构而非主张内容决定：各主张间拒绝率仅波动11个百分点，而单个提示框架变化可使模型内拒绝率波动近100个百分点，切换请求的谬误类型可使显式框架内拒绝率波动超80个百分点。教育辩论教练提示框架将所有四个模型家族的拒绝率降至接近零，但绕过行为并非干净合规——模型通常产生标记合规，即在同一响应中命名所请求的操纵手法。四个模型在拒绝、标记合规、软拒绝和干净合规上的分布各不相同。

Conclusion: 当前的安全后训练未能有效约束LLM生成修辞谬误的能力，且这种能力可通过简单的提示框架操纵被显著激发。安全对齐对请求结构的敏感性远高于主张内容，说明现有的安全防御存在结构性漏洞，需要更稳健的对策。

Abstract: Whether large language models can be prompted to generate rhetorical fallacies on demand, and whether current safety post-training constrains this behavior, has received less attention than the related question of detecting fallacies in existing text. We close this gap with DeflectBench, evaluating 23,990 generations from four frontier models across three deflection strategies (whataboutism, ad hominem, red herring), seven prompt framings, and 80 claims spanning four controversy levels. Refusal is governed primarily by request structure rather than claim content. Per claim refusal varies by only 11 percentage points across the 80 claims, while a single prompt frame change can swing within model refusal by nearly 100 percentage points and switching the requested fallacy type can swing it by over 80 percentage points within explicit framings. An educational debate coach prompt framing collapses refusal to near zero across all four model families, but the bypassed behavior is not clean compliance. Models typically produce labeled compliance, naming the requested manipulation in the same response that contains it. The four models distribute differently across refusal, labeled compliance, soft refusal, and clean compliance. The code and dataset are released at https://github.com/ArtKanke/DeflectBench.

</details>


### [97] [Recipes for Steering and Scaling LLMs via Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26120)
*Jiajun He,Zongyu Guo,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Yuanqi Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个灵活且理论扎实的框架，用于通过采样引导和扩展自回归大语言模型（LLM）。该框架包含两种算法——基于序列蒙特卡洛（SMC）和副本交换（RE）——可将生成过程引导至基模型的幂、乘积或倾斜分布。实验表明，该方法在无需外部监督或奖励模型的情况下，比Best-of-N和标准MCMC基线具有更好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM是概率模型，通常通过自回归分解定义。尽管已有工作开始研究超越基模型的更丰富目标分布，但现有的采样策略效率很低。作者旨在提供一种系统性的框架，以高效地引导和扩展LLM的采样过程。

Method: 提出了一个理论上扎实的框架来引导和扩展自回归LLM的采样。框架内包含两种算法：1) 基于序列蒙特卡洛（SMC）的算法；2) 基于副本交换（RE）的算法。这两种算法能将生成过程引导向基模型分布的幂、乘积或倾斜（tilting）形式。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在扩展生成质量方面比Best-of-N和标准MCMC基线具有更优的扩展性，且无需外部监督或奖励模型。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一个通过采样对LLM进行概率推断的系统性配方，展示了在无监督条件下提升LLM生成质量的可行性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are probabilistic models, typically defined by an autoregressive factorization. While recent work has begun to study richer target distributions beyond the base model, the sampling strategies remain highly inefficient. In this paper, we present a flexible and theoretically grounded framework for steering and scaling autoregressive LLMs with sampling. Within this framework, we describe two algorithms -- one based on Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) and one based on Replica Exchange (RE) -- that steer generation toward powering, product or tilting of the base model distribution. We illustrate this framework through scaling the generation quality of LLMs without external supervision or reward models. Experimental results demonstrate our methods scale more favorably than Best-of-N and standard MCMC baselines. Overall, this paper offers a systematic recipe for probabilistic inference with LLMs via sampling.

</details>


### [98] [Can a Model Catch Its Own Hallucinations for Free?: Label-Free Doubt Signals Hold Their Own Against a Labelled Dataset for Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26121)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标签的模型拒答（abstention）训练方法：利用模型自身的置信度（概率）作为监督信号，当高置信度时回答、低置信度时拒答，无需人工标注正确/错误答案。在六个开源模型（1B-8B，两类架构）的短问答任务上，该方法与使用标签监督的拒答微调性能无统计显著差异，证明了模型自身"怀疑"可近似替代标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常以同样流利的方式陈述错误事实，但模型内部往往"知道"自身信心不足——其赋予答案的概率在错误事实处会偏低。传统做法要求识别并缩小正确答案标签来训练拒答，需要标注数据集。作者希望探究：模型的置信度信号是否可免费替代标注标签，实现无监督的拒答训练。

Method: 使用LoRA微调每个模型，以冻结的模型置信度作为唯一监督信号：当置信度高时训练模型作答，置信度低时训练其回答"我不确定"，完全不用正确性标签。评估采用独立裁判模型判定回答对错，在六个开源模型（1B-8B，两个模型家族）上进行短问答实验，并设置对照组（训练模型强化困难样本而非拒答）以区分校准收益与死记硬背。

Result: 在匹配的覆盖率（coverage）条件下，无需标签的置信度驱动拒答训练与标签监督的拒答微调之间无统计显著差异。对照组（强化困难样本而非拒答）未见增益，表明收益来自校准而非死记硬背。该方法唯一盲区是模型高置信但错误的事实，无法通过该信号识别。

Conclusion: 模型自身的"怀疑"（置信度）可作为标注数据集近乎免费的替代品，用于训练模型学会何时拒答。这降低了拒答训练对人工标注的依赖，但无法处理高置信度错误这一固有盲区，指出了置信度信号的能力边界。

Abstract: Large language models state false facts as fluently as true ones, yet a model often "knows" internally when it is on shaky ground: the probability it assigns to its own answer tends to dip on the facts it gets wrong. The usual way to act on this, teaching a model to abstain rather than guess, requires a labelled dataset of right and wrong answers. We ask whether the model's own confidence, which is free and needs no labels, can do that job instead. We fine-tune each model (with LoRA) to answer when its frozen confidence is high and to say "I'm not sure" when it is low, using the signal alone and no correctness labels. Across six open-weights models (1B-8B, two families) on short-form factual question answering, with correctness adjudicated by an independent judge model, this label-free recipe holds its own against label-supervised abstention-tuning: at matched coverage we find no statistically detectable difference between the two. A control that drills hard examples instead of abstaining does not help, indicating the gain comes from calibration, not rote memorization. The signal's one blind spot is confidently wrong facts, which it cannot flag. A model's own doubt is thus a near-free substitute for a labelled dataset when teaching it when to abstain. Code and artifacts are available on request.

</details>


### [99] [Which India Survives Translation? Narrative Homogenisation Across Indian Oral Traditions in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26123)
*Paarth Singh Rathore*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对三种截然不同的印度地区口头及文学传统（拉贾斯坦Pabuji史诗、古典泰米尔Sangam诗歌、孟加拉民间故事）进行了试点计算分析，通过比较LLM生成文本与真实参考语料之间的语义距离，发现模型存在部分文化同质化现象，并意外发现使用区域语言提示反而比英语提示更降低了对真实传统的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型主要基于英语互联网文本训练，过度代表某些文化叙事，可能将非西方叙事传统的多样性压缩为单一同质化原型。现有研究缺乏对具体小众口头传统的定量检验，需要一种轻量级、可扩展的计算方法来评估这一现象。

Method: 收集三种传统的真实参考语料（分别为11、21、10段），对两个LLM（Claude Sonnet和Gemini）提出54个生成请求，涵盖每传统的三种提示类型（通用、文化特定、区域语言）。使用Sentence-BERT嵌入和余弦相似度衡量参考漂移（输出与其自身传统真实文本的接近程度）和跨传统趋同度。

Result: 输出虽更接近自身传统参考而非其他传统，但跨传统相似度（0.52-0.66）相对于传统间真实距离所预期的水平偏高，表明存在部分同质化。意外发现区域语言提示（印地语、泰米尔语或孟加拉语）反而一致降低了保真度，对拉贾斯坦和孟加拉传统最多降低27个百分点。

Conclusion: 模型存在部分文化同质化问题；区域语言提示比英语提示更不利于保真，反映多语言提示在"激发一般文化多样性"与"模拟单一狭隘且文献记录较少的口头传统"之间的区别。该试点研究可作为大规模人工标注研究的轻量级补充。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained predominantly on English-language internet text that over-represents certain cultural narratives, raising concerns that models flatten the diversity of non-Western storytelling traditions into a single homogenized archetype. We present a pilot computational study examining this across three maximally distinct Indian regional oral and literary traditions: the Rajasthani Pabuji epic, classical Tamil Sangam poetry, and Bengali folk tales. We collected authentic reference corpora for each tradition (11, 21, and 10 passages respectively) and prompted two LLMs (Claude Sonnet and Gemini) with 54 generation requests spanning three prompt types per tradition - generic, culturally specific, and regional-language. Using Sentence-BERT embeddings and cosine similarity, we measure reference drift (how closely outputs track their own tradition's authentic texts relative to the other two) and cross-tradition convergence (how similar outputs are across traditions). We find that while outputs remain closer to their own tradition's reference than to others, cross-tradition similarity is high (0.52-0.66) relative to what the traditions' genuine distance would predict, indicating partial homogenisation. Unexpectedly, prompting in the regional language (Hindi, Tamil, or Bengali) consistently reduced fidelity to the authentic tradition relative to English prompting, by as much as 27 percentage points for Rajasthani and Bengali traditions. We discuss this against conflicting prior results on multilingual prompting and argue it reflects a difference between eliciting general cultural diversity and simulating one narrow, lesser-documented oral tradition. We position this pilot as a lightweight, scalable complement to recent large-scale human-annotation studies of Indian cultural misrepresentation in LLM-generated stories, as part of a broader doctoral research program.

</details>


### [100] [Natural-Language Policies to Executable Decisions: An Interpretable Large Language Model Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26124)
*Ziqiang Zhang,Jing Ma,Zilong Wang,Jiayuan Chen,Yi Qiao,Yu He,Wei Zhang,Dai Cheng,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套生产级LLM驱动的旅游定价系统，采用"LLM做结构化提取与有界策略/路径选择，数值定价由确定性引擎执行"的严格决策边界架构。策略被编译为可解释的条件树，支持开放式策略演进且无需改代码，并通过周期性微调提升树归纳与路径匹配能力。系统在7个景区、12个业务类别、1,500+运营商和1,000+活跃策略的生产环境中部署，六个月处理3,960个订单，将订单管理团队从15-20人缩减至3人，单订单处理时长从10分钟降至2分钟以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模旅游定价自动化面临双重挑战：旅游订单结构高度非结构化，而定价策略复杂、快速演化且本质上是开放式的（无法预先穷举）。传统规则引擎脆弱且维护成本高，而完全不受约束的LLM智能体在金融决策场景中缺乏可靠性和可审计性，因此需要一种兼顾灵活性与确定性的定价方案。
